AWS SAA-C03 - Supplied Book Questions with Reviewed Explanations
Source: James Howlett; PDF supplied by the user. Editorial review: 14 October 2026.
The original question and key are preserved. Practice corrections are explicitly shown.
Review-only items have no defensible unique scored answer as written, or are outside current practice scope.
For those items, option notes explain applicability instead of falsely labeling all alternatives wrong.
400 imported items: 247 reviewed, 45 corrected, 108 review-only.
Scored-eligible imported items: 292. Related concept families remain identifiable.

==============================================================================
1. Which AWS service allows you to create a virtual private network that extends an on-premises network into the AWS cloud?
Bank ID: H001 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Amazon VPC Peering
B. AWS VPN
C. AWS Direct Connect
D. Amazon Route 53

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: Site-to-Site VPN establishes IPsec tunnels between an on-premises network and a VPC over existing network connectivity. Direct Connect instead provisions a dedicated link; VPC peering connects VPCs, and Route 53 handles DNS.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. VPC peering connects compatible VPCs and is not transitive. It is not an on-premises VPN, encryption feature or application credential service. This item tests encrypted hybrid tunnels rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. Site-to-Site VPN establishes IPsec tunnels between an on-premises network and a VPC over existing network connectivity. Direct Connect instead provisions a dedicated link; VPC peering connects VPCs, and Route 53 handles DNS.
C: Less suitable for this question. Direct Connect supplies dedicated private hybrid connectivity, not encryption by default. A private circuit is not a general internet CDN or migration tool by itself. This item tests encrypted hybrid tunnels rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Route 53 provides authoritative DNS and supported routing policies. Each policy has distinct selection rules; DNS routing does not provision application copies or replicate data. This item tests encrypted hybrid tunnels rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: encrypted hybrid tunnels - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/vpn/latest/s2svpn/VPC_VPN.html

==============================================================================
2. In a VPC, what component allows for inbound and outbound traffic control at the subnet level?
Bank ID: H002 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Network ACL
B. Security Group
C. NAT Gateway
D. Internet Gateway

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: Network ACLs apply stateless, ordered allow and deny rules at subnet boundaries. They can explicitly deny a source CIDR. Security groups instead provide stateful allow rules at resource network interfaces.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. Network ACLs apply stateless, ordered allow and deny rules at subnet boundaries. They can explicitly deny a source CIDR. Security groups instead provide stateful allow rules at resource network interfaces.
B: Less suitable for this question. Security groups provide stateful interface-level allow rules for network traffic. They do not have explicit deny rules or implement application user permissions. This item tests subnet-level network filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. NAT enables specified outbound address translation for private resources. It is not an inbound authorization system, a global routing service or a data encryption feature. This item tests subnet-level network filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. An internet gateway supports VPC internet routing with suitable routes and addressing. It does not encrypt storage or authorize S3 data access. This item tests subnet-level network filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: subnet-level network filtering - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/vpc/latest/userguide/vpc-network-acls.html

3. What is the primary purpose of an AWS Identity and Access Management (IAM) role?
Bank ID: H003 | Official task: 1.1 | Review: Corrected
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. To provide persistent credentials for users
B. To allow services to interact securely without using credentials
C. To define virtual private network routes
D. To enable detailed billing reports

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: IAM roles define assumable permissions and provide temporary security credentials. They do not eliminate credentials: AWS SDKs can retrieve and refresh role credentials without embedding long-lived keys. The supplied option must be corrected. Practice option wording corrected; original options preserved below.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. An assumed role supplies temporary credentials; persistent credentials belong to a different credential model. This item tests temporary role credentials rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Source wording corrected. Role-based access still uses temporary security credentials; it avoids distributing long-lived credentials, not all credentials. Reviewed replacement: To provide assumable permissions and temporary security credentials instead of embedded long-term credentials. IAM roles define assumable permissions and provide temporary security credentials. They do not eliminate credentials: AWS SDKs can retrieve and refresh role credentials without embedding long-lived keys. The supplied option must be corrected. Practice option wording corrected; original options preserved below.
C: Less suitable for this question. Route-table entries determine network packet paths. IAM roles define assumable permissions and credentials, not those network routes. This item tests temporary role credentials rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Billing reports describe usage and charges. Enabling them is not the defining purpose of an IAM role. This item tests temporary role credentials rather than that alternative mechanism.

Practice version:
What is the primary purpose of an AWS Identity and Access Management (IAM) role?
A. To provide persistent credentials for users
B. To provide assumable permissions and temporary security credentials instead of embedded long-term credentials
C. To define virtual private network routes
D. To enable detailed billing reports
Change: Practice option wording corrected; original options preserved below.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: temporary role credentials - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/IAM/latest/UserGuide/id_roles.html

4. How can you ensure that sensitive data stored in Amazon S3 is encrypted at rest?
Bank ID: H004 | Official task: 1.3 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Use AWS Shield
B. Enable Server-Side Encryption (SSE) on S3
C. Use an Internet Gateway
D. Create a VPC Peering connection

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: S3 server-side encryption protects object data at rest. New S3 uploads are already encrypted with SSE-S3 by default; an explicit bucket configuration can choose SSE-KMS when key control is required. Networking and versioning do not perform encryption.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. Shield addresses distributed denial-of-service attacks, not general IAM authorization, key management or every application exploit. This item tests S3 encryption at rest rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. S3 server-side encryption protects object data at rest. New S3 uploads are already encrypted with SSE-S3 by default; an explicit bucket configuration can choose SSE-KMS when key control is required. Networking and versioning do not perform encryption.
C: Less suitable for this question. An internet gateway supports VPC internet routing with suitable routes and addressing. It does not encrypt storage or authorize S3 data access. This item tests S3 encryption at rest rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. VPC peering connects compatible VPCs and is not transitive. It is not an on-premises VPN, encryption feature or application credential service. This item tests S3 encryption at rest rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: S3 encryption at rest - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/default-bucket-encryption.html

5. Which service helps mitigate DDoS attacks on AWS resources?
Bank ID: H005 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS Trusted Advisor
B. AWS Config
C. AWS Shield
D. Amazon SNS

Book answer: C
Reviewed answer: C
Review rationale: AWS Shield provides DDoS protections. Shield Standard is automatic, while Shield Advanced adds paid capabilities. WAF focuses on HTTP request rules, and monitoring or access policies do not substitute for DDoS mitigation.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. Trusted Advisor provides supported best-practice and cost checks. It recommends actions rather than automatically eliminating every unused resource. This item tests DDoS mitigation rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Less suitable for this question. Config records supported resource configurations and evaluates rules; configured remediation is separate. It is detective rather than a universal preventive permission firewall. This item tests DDoS mitigation rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Correct. AWS Shield provides DDoS protections. Shield Standard is automatic, while Shield Advanced adds paid capabilities. WAF focuses on HTTP request rules, and monitoring or access policies do not substitute for DDoS mitigation.
D: Less suitable for this question. SNS provides pub-sub distribution to subscriptions. A worker queue such as SQS adds independent durable consumer buffering and retry handling. This item tests DDoS mitigation rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: DDoS mitigation - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/waf/latest/developerguide/shield-chapter.html

6. You want to allow only specific IAM users to access an S3 bucket. What is the best way to control this access?
Bank ID: H006 | Official task: 1.1 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Enable MFA Delete on the bucket
B. Create a bucket policy that grants access to those users
C. Create a NAT Gateway to restrict access
D. Use VPC Endpoints

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: A bucket policy can grant or deny specified principals access to the bucket and its objects. The effective decision also depends on other applicable policies and explicit denies; merely adding an allow does not remove every other principal's permissions.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. S3 MFA Delete protects certain bucket-versioning and permanent-version-deletion operations; it does not grant or deny ordinary object reads to a list of users. This item tests S3 principal authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. A bucket policy can grant or deny specified principals access to the bucket and its objects. The effective decision also depends on other applicable policies and explicit denies; merely adding an allow does not remove every other principal's permissions.
C: Less suitable for this question. NAT enables specified outbound address translation for private resources. It is not an inbound authorization system, a global routing service or a data encryption feature. This item tests S3 principal authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. VPC endpoints provide private paths to supported services. IAM and TLS requirements still apply; interface endpoints and gateway endpoints have different costs and capabilities. This item tests S3 principal authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: S3 principal authorization - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/bucket-policies.html

7. Which feature allows secure access to AWS resources without requiring a public IP address?
Bank ID: H007 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. NAT Instance
B. VPC Peering
C. AWS Direct Connect
D. VPC Endpoint

Book answer: D
Reviewed answer: D
Review rationale: VPC endpoints let eligible services be reached privately without assigning public IPs to the calling instances. The endpoint type and supported service determine routing. Endpoints still require IAM authorization and do not themselves guarantee encryption.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. NAT enables specified outbound address translation for private resources. It is not an inbound authorization system, a global routing service or a data encryption feature. This item tests private AWS service endpoints rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Less suitable for this question. VPC peering connects compatible VPCs and is not transitive. It is not an on-premises VPN, encryption feature or application credential service. This item tests private AWS service endpoints rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. Direct Connect supplies dedicated private hybrid connectivity, not encryption by default. A private circuit is not a general internet CDN or migration tool by itself. This item tests private AWS service endpoints rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Correct. VPC endpoints let eligible services be reached privately without assigning public IPs to the calling instances. The endpoint type and supported service determine routing. Endpoints still require IAM authorization and do not themselves guarantee encryption.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: private AWS service endpoints - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/vpc/latest/privatelink/privatelink-access-aws-services.html

8. How can you restrict specific IP addresses from accessing your Amazon EC2 instance?
Bank ID: H008 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Modify the Network ACLs
B. Create an Elastic IP for the instance
C. Enable Auto Scaling
D. Set up an S3 bucket policy

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: Network ACLs apply stateless, ordered allow and deny rules at subnet boundaries. They can explicitly deny a source CIDR. Security groups instead provide stateful allow rules at resource network interfaces.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. Network ACLs apply stateless, ordered allow and deny rules at subnet boundaries. They can explicitly deny a source CIDR. Security groups instead provide stateful allow rules at resource network interfaces.
B: Less suitable for this question. An Elastic IP is a public address resource, not an IP filtering policy or a durable application state store. This item tests subnet-level network filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. EC2 Auto Scaling changes and maintains group instance capacity using configured policies. It does not simply resize live instances vertically, act as a load balancer or operate one group across Regions. This item tests subnet-level network filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. S3 resource policies and access-point policies scope supported object access. A policy is not encryption and does not automatically revoke every separate applicable allow. This item tests subnet-level network filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: subnet-level network filtering - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/vpc/latest/userguide/vpc-network-acls.html

9. Which AWS service provides built-in DDoS protection for Amazon CloudFront and Amazon Route 53?
Bank ID: H009 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Corrected
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS WAF
B. AWS Shield Advanced
C. AWS Config
D. AWS VPN

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: The included baseline protection is AWS Shield Standard, not the optional paid Shield Advanced tier. CloudFront and Route 53 benefit from AWS's built-in DDoS protections. The supplied option name is corrected while retaining its letter. Practice option wording corrected; original options preserved below.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. WAF filters HTTP requests, including application attacks. Built-in AWS DDoS protection is the Shield Standard tier. This item tests automatic DDoS protection rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Source wording corrected. Shield Advanced is an optional paid DDoS tier; baseline built-in protection is Shield Standard. Reviewed replacement: AWS Shield Standard. The included baseline protection is AWS Shield Standard, not the optional paid Shield Advanced tier. CloudFront and Route 53 benefit from AWS's built-in DDoS protections. The supplied option name is corrected while retaining its letter. Practice option wording corrected; original options preserved below.
C: Less suitable for this question. Config evaluates resource configurations; it is not the DDoS mitigation layer. This item tests automatic DDoS protection rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. VPN creates encrypted tunnels; it is not automatic DDoS protection for CloudFront and Route 53. This item tests automatic DDoS protection rather than that alternative mechanism.

Practice version:
Which AWS service provides built-in DDoS protection for Amazon CloudFront and Amazon Route 53?
A. AWS WAF
B. AWS Shield Standard
C. AWS Config
D. AWS VPN
Change: Practice option wording corrected; original options preserved below.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: automatic DDoS protection - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/waf/latest/developerguide/shield-chapter.html

10. What mechanism can you use to rotate encryption keys in AWS KMS automatically?
Bank ID: H010 | Official task: 1.3 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Use AWS Systems Manager
B. Enable key rotation in KMS
C. Set up a CloudWatch alarm for key rotation
D. Use IAM roles to manage key rotation

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: Enable automatic rotation for eligible KMS keys. KMS keeps old key material for decrypting earlier ciphertext. Eligibility and configurable rotation behavior depend on the key type; an IAM role or alarm does not rotate key material.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. Systems Manager supports fleet operations and configured automation. It can run custom workflows but is not a built-in universal snapshot optimizer or every security enforcement mechanism. This item tests KMS key-material rotation rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. Enable automatic rotation for eligible KMS keys. KMS keeps old key material for decrypting earlier ciphertext. Eligibility and configurable rotation behavior depend on the key type; an IAM role or alarm does not rotate key material.
C: Less suitable for this question. KMS rotation changes eligible key material while preserving old decryption material; alarms or identity assignments do not perform that rotation. This item tests KMS key-material rotation rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. KMS rotation changes eligible key material while preserving old decryption material; alarms or identity assignments do not perform that rotation. This item tests KMS key-material rotation rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: KMS key-material rotation - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/kms/latest/developerguide/rotate-keys.html

11. What AWS service provides a way to monitor API requests and responses for security auditing?
Bank ID: H011 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS CloudTrail
B. AWS GuardDuty
C. AWS CloudWatch
D. AWS Config

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: CloudTrail records supported AWS API activity, including caller information, actions and request details. Configure the needed management/data events and durable trails for the required coverage. It is not a packet capture or a guarantee that every application action is logged.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. CloudTrail records supported AWS API activity, including caller information, actions and request details. Configure the needed management/data events and durable trails for the required coverage. It is not a packet capture or a guarantee that every application action is logged.
B: Less suitable for this question. GuardDuty detects threats using supported telemetry and publishes findings. Response actions need a separately configured investigation/remediation workflow. This item tests AWS API auditing rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. Metrics, logs and alarms observe behavior and can trigger configured actions. Monitoring alone does not scale resources, enforce identity permissions, encrypt data or create replicas. This item tests AWS API auditing rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Config records supported resource configurations and evaluates rules; configured remediation is separate. It is detective rather than a universal preventive permission firewall. This item tests AWS API auditing rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: AWS API auditing - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/awscloudtrail/latest/userguide/cloudtrail-user-guide.html

12. How can you protect data in transit between AWS services?
Bank ID: H012 | Official task: 1.3 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Use AWS WAF
B. Enable encryption on data transfer
C. Use VPC Endpoints
D. Apply IAM policies

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: TLS encrypts and integrity-protects supported client/service connections. Network isolation and IAM permissions complement TLS but do not encrypt bytes in transit by themselves. Endpoints and clients must actually use the secure protocol.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. WAF applies HTTP request rules on supported web endpoints, including supported attack-pattern and geographic matching. It is not a blanket control for every AWS resource or all protocols. This item tests transport encryption rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. TLS encrypts and integrity-protects supported client/service connections. Network isolation and IAM permissions complement TLS but do not encrypt bytes in transit by themselves. Endpoints and clients must actually use the secure protocol.
C: Less suitable for this question. VPC endpoints provide private paths to supported services. IAM and TLS requirements still apply; interface endpoints and gateway endpoints have different costs and capabilities. This item tests transport encryption rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. IAM expresses who can perform supported actions on resources. Authorization does not by itself encrypt traffic, record all application logs or route network packets. This item tests transport encryption rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: transport encryption - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/security-best-practices.html

13. Which AWS service allows for secure, centralized management of encryption keys?
Bank ID: H013 | Official task: 1.3 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS Key Management Service (KMS)
B. AWS Secrets Manager
C. AWS Certificate Manager
D. AWS Systems Manager Parameter Store

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: KMS manages cryptographic keys and integrates with services such as DynamoDB for encryption at rest. Secrets Manager stores secret values, ACM handles certificates, and WAF filters web requests. Encryption does not replace access authorization.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. KMS manages cryptographic keys and integrates with services such as DynamoDB for encryption at rest. Secrets Manager stores secret values, ACM handles certificates, and WAF filters web requests. Encryption does not replace access authorization.
B: Less suitable for this question. Secrets Manager stores protected secret values and supports configured secret rotation; it does not replace network or application authorization. This item tests managed encryption keys rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. ACM manages TLS certificates for supported integrations; certificates must be deployed to TLS endpoints and do not authorize every caller. This item tests managed encryption keys rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Parameter Store stores configuration and optionally encrypted SecureString values; managed secret rotation is a different requirement. This item tests managed encryption keys rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: managed encryption keys - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/kms/latest/developerguide/overview.html

14. What is the purpose of an IAM policy?
Bank ID: H014 | Official task: 1.1 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. To control which users can access a VPC
B. To define the permissions that a user or group has
C. To encrypt data at rest
D. To monitor AWS resource usage

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: IAM policies express allowed or denied actions on resources, subject to conditions and policy evaluation. Roles carry permissions and are assumed by trusted principals. Network controls cannot substitute for resource/action authorization.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. VPC defines isolated virtual networking, routes and subnet boundaries. It does not automatically make every appliance or application dependency redundant. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. IAM policies express allowed or denied actions on resources, subject to conditions and policy evaluation. Roles carry permissions and are assumed by trusted principals. Network controls cannot substitute for resource/action authorization.
C: Less suitable for this question. Encryption protects data confidentiality under a key/transport model. It does not itself recover deleted objects, distribute traffic, scale capacity or prove the caller is authorized. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Observability collects evidence and helps diagnose faults. Alarms or dashboards need configured actions to change capacity, enforce access or restore data. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: identity authorization - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/IAM/latest/UserGuide/id_roles.html

15. To secure access to your AWS Management Console, which feature should you enable for added security?
Bank ID: H015 | Official task: 1.1 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS SSO
B. IAM Roles
C. MFA (Multi-Factor Authentication)
D. IAM Policies

Book answer: C
Reviewed answer: C
Review rationale: MFA adds another authentication factor beyond a password. It reduces the risk from a stolen password, but users still need legitimate identities and appropriate permissions. Federation and roles can be part of the design rather than competing replacements for MFA.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. IAM Identity Center (formerly AWS SSO) centrally manages workforce access assignments; it is not a data encryption or network routing feature. This item tests stronger console authentication rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Less suitable for this question. IAM expresses who can perform supported actions on resources. Authorization does not by itself encrypt traffic, record all application logs or route network packets. This item tests stronger console authentication rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Correct. MFA adds another authentication factor beyond a password. It reduces the risk from a stolen password, but users still need legitimate identities and appropriate permissions. Federation and roles can be part of the design rather than competing replacements for MFA.
D: Less suitable for this question. IAM expresses who can perform supported actions on resources. Authorization does not by itself encrypt traffic, record all application logs or route network packets. This item tests stronger console authentication rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: stronger console authentication - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/IAM/latest/UserGuide/id_credentials_mfa.html

16. Which AWS service can be used to scan your AWS environment for compliance with security best practices?
Bank ID: H016 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS Inspector
B. AWS Config
C. AWS GuardDuty
D. AWS CloudTrail

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: AWS Config rules evaluate resource configurations against desired compliance requirements. Inspector specializes in supported workload vulnerabilities; GuardDuty detects threats; CloudTrail records API history. Define appropriate Config rules rather than expecting every security standard to be evaluated automatically.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. Inspector assesses supported software vulnerabilities and exposure. It does not replace request filtering, packet telemetry or general backup management. This item tests configuration compliance evaluation rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. AWS Config rules evaluate resource configurations against desired compliance requirements. Inspector specializes in supported workload vulnerabilities; GuardDuty detects threats; CloudTrail records API history. Define appropriate Config rules rather than expecting every security standard to be evaluated automatically.
C: Less suitable for this question. GuardDuty detects threats using supported telemetry and publishes findings. Response actions need a separately configured investigation/remediation workflow. This item tests configuration compliance evaluation rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. CloudTrail records supported account API activity under configured event coverage. It is not ordinary application performance monitoring or a full packet capture. This item tests configuration compliance evaluation rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: configuration compliance evaluation - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/config/latest/developerguide/evaluate-config.html

17. How can you ensure that all data written to an S3 bucket is encrypted?
Bank ID: H017 | Official task: 1.3 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Apply encryption at the bucket level
B. Use S3 Access Points
C. Configure lifecycle policies
D. Enable versioning

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: S3 server-side encryption protects object data at rest. New S3 uploads are already encrypted with SSE-S3 by default; an explicit bucket configuration can choose SSE-KMS when key control is required. Networking and versioning do not perform encryption.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. S3 server-side encryption protects object data at rest. New S3 uploads are already encrypted with SSE-S3 by default; an explicit bucket configuration can choose SSE-KMS when key control is required. Networking and versioning do not perform encryption.
B: Less suitable for this question. S3 resource policies and access-point policies scope supported object access. A policy is not encryption and does not automatically revoke every separate applicable allow. This item tests S3 encryption at rest rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. S3 lifecycle rules manage transitions and expiration using configured rules such as object age. They do not by themselves monitor every object's access frequency. This item tests S3 encryption at rest rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Versioning preserves object versions to recover overwritten/deleted data. It does not encrypt bytes, lower transfer costs or create cross-Region copies by itself. This item tests S3 encryption at rest rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: S3 encryption at rest - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/default-bucket-encryption.html

18. What is the purpose of AWS Security Hub?
Bank ID: H018 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. To provide a unified view of security alerts and compliance status
B. To manage security group rules
C. To store encryption keys
D. To handle disaster recovery

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: Security Hub aggregates supported security findings and provides security posture/compliance views across integrated services and accounts. It does not replace the source detectors or automatically remediate every finding.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. Security Hub aggregates supported security findings and provides security posture/compliance views across integrated services and accounts. It does not replace the source detectors or automatically remediate every finding.
B: Less suitable for this question. Security groups provide stateful interface-level allow rules for network traffic. They do not have explicit deny rules or implement application user permissions. This item tests aggregated security posture rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. KMS manages cryptographic keys and operations for supported service encryption; it does not itself supply a complete authorization, network or backup architecture. This item tests aggregated security posture rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Disaster recovery requires recoverable data, recreateable infrastructure and tested traffic recovery against RPO/RTO. A service name alone is not a complete recovery strategy. This item tests aggregated security posture rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: aggregated security posture - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/securityhub/latest/userguide/what-is-securityhub.html

19. How can you restrict access to an Amazon RDS instance based on the IP address?
Bank ID: H019 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Modify the RDS instance parameter group
B. Use security groups to control inbound traffic
C. Enable IAM roles for RDS
D. Configure CloudTrail

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: Security groups allow specified protocols, ports and source/destination CIDRs or security groups at network interfaces. They are stateful and have no explicit deny rules; omit unwanted sources and audit all attached groups.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. RDS manages supported relational database engines with optional HA and read-scaling configurations. Native backups, standby failover and read replicas are different mechanisms. This item tests stateful resource traffic filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. Security groups allow specified protocols, ports and source/destination CIDRs or security groups at network interfaces. They are stateful and have no explicit deny rules; omit unwanted sources and audit all attached groups.
C: Less suitable for this question. IAM expresses who can perform supported actions on resources. Authorization does not by itself encrypt traffic, record all application logs or route network packets. This item tests stateful resource traffic filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. CloudTrail records supported account API activity under configured event coverage. It is not ordinary application performance monitoring or a full packet capture. This item tests stateful resource traffic filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: stateful resource traffic filtering - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/vpc/latest/userguide/vpc-security-groups.html

20. Which AWS service helps you enforce security policies for AWS resources?
Bank ID: H020 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Corrected
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS CloudFormation
B. AWS Config
C. AWS Secrets Manager
D. AWS CloudTrail

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: Config rules identify noncompliant resource configurations and can invoke configured remediation. Config is primarily detective; it is not a blanket preventive permission system. The practice wording explicitly asks for evaluation and configured remediation. Practice stem clarified; original preserved below.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. CloudFormation provisions and updates declared resource stacks from templates. A template must encode the desired resilience; infrastructure automation is not automatically a database backup or code delivery pipeline. This item tests configuration checks and remediation rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. Config rules identify noncompliant resource configurations and can invoke configured remediation. Config is primarily detective; it is not a blanket preventive permission system. The practice wording explicitly asks for evaluation and configured remediation.
C: Less suitable for this question. Secrets Manager stores protected secret values and supports configured secret rotation; it does not replace network or application authorization. This item tests configuration checks and remediation rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. CloudTrail records supported account API activity under configured event coverage. It is not ordinary application performance monitoring or a full packet capture. This item tests configuration checks and remediation rather than that alternative mechanism.

Practice version:
Which AWS service evaluates supported resource configurations against security rules and can trigger configured remediation?
A. AWS CloudFormation
B. AWS Config
C. AWS Secrets Manager
D. AWS CloudTrail
Change: Practice stem clarified; original preserved below.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: configuration checks and remediation - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/config/latest/developerguide/evaluate-config.html

21. What is the function of a Security Group in AWS?
Bank ID: H021 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. To restrict the amount of data transferred
B. To manage encryption keys
C. To control inbound and outbound traffic to instances
D. To automate resource scaling

Book answer: C
Reviewed answer: C
Review rationale: Security groups allow specified protocols, ports and source/destination CIDRs or security groups at network interfaces. They are stateful and have no explicit deny rules; omit unwanted sources and audit all attached groups.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. Security groups filter allowed connections; they do not impose a metered amount of transferred data. This item tests stateful resource traffic filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Less suitable for this question. Encryption keys are managed through cryptographic services such as KMS, not network interface rules. This item tests stateful resource traffic filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Correct. Security groups allow specified protocols, ports and source/destination CIDRs or security groups at network interfaces. They are stateful and have no explicit deny rules; omit unwanted sources and audit all attached groups.
D: Less suitable for this question. Scaling policies change capacity; a security group only controls allowed traffic. This item tests stateful resource traffic filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: stateful resource traffic filtering - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/vpc/latest/userguide/vpc-security-groups.html

22. Which AWS service allows you to manage SSL/TLS certificates for your domain names?
Bank ID: H022 | Official task: 1.3 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS KMS
B. AWS Secrets Manager
C. AWS Certificate Manager
D. AWS CloudHSM

Book answer: C
Reviewed answer: C
Review rationale: ACM manages supported SSL/TLS certificates for integrated endpoints. A certificate must be attached to the endpoint and a TLS connection used; obtaining a certificate alone does not encrypt every network connection.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. KMS manages cryptographic keys and operations for supported service encryption; it does not itself supply a complete authorization, network or backup architecture. This item tests TLS certificate management rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Less suitable for this question. Secrets Manager stores protected secret values and supports configured secret rotation; it does not replace network or application authorization. This item tests TLS certificate management rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Correct. ACM manages supported SSL/TLS certificates for integrated endpoints. A certificate must be attached to the endpoint and a TLS connection used; obtaining a certificate alone does not encrypt every network connection.
D: Less suitable for this question. CloudHSM supplies dedicated cryptographic hardware and customer-controlled key management, not general application policy enforcement. This item tests TLS certificate management rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: TLS certificate management - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/acm/latest/userguide/acm-overview.html

23. What is AWS IAM’s primary function?
Bank ID: H023 | Official task: 1.1 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. To manage and secure network configurations
B. To monitor API activity
C. To provide access control for AWS resources
D. To handle data replication

Book answer: C
Reviewed answer: C
Review rationale: IAM policies express allowed or denied actions on resources, subject to conditions and policy evaluation. Roles carry permissions and are assumed by trusted principals. Network controls cannot substitute for resource/action authorization.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. VPC defines isolated virtual networking, routes and subnet boundaries. It does not automatically make every appliance or application dependency redundant. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Less suitable for this question. CloudTrail records supported account API activity under configured event coverage. It is not ordinary application performance monitoring or a full packet capture. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Correct. IAM policies express allowed or denied actions on resources, subject to conditions and policy evaluation. Roles carry permissions and are assumed by trusted principals. Network controls cannot substitute for resource/action authorization.
D: Less suitable for this question. Replication maintains copies under a specific mechanism and consistency model. It is not interchangeable with backups, encryption or any one service's native storage durability. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: identity authorization - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/IAM/latest/UserGuide/id_roles.html

24. To ensure that sensitive information in environment variables is protected, which AWS service should be used?
Bank ID: H024 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Corrected
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS Secrets Manager
B. AWS Systems Manager Parameter Store
C. AWS KMS
D. AWS CloudFormation

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: Secrets Manager stores protected application secrets and supports configured rotation. Parameter Store SecureString is also valid for secure values, so the practice question adds a managed-rotation requirement to distinguish the choices. Practice stem clarified; original preserved below.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. Secrets Manager stores protected application secrets and supports configured rotation. Parameter Store SecureString is also valid for secure values, so the practice question adds a managed-rotation requirement to distinguish the choices.
B: Less suitable for this question. Parameter Store stores configuration and optionally encrypted SecureString values; managed secret rotation is a different requirement. This item tests application secret rotation rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. KMS manages cryptographic keys and operations for supported service encryption; it does not itself supply a complete authorization, network or backup architecture. This item tests application secret rotation rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. CloudFormation provisions and updates declared resource stacks from templates. A template must encode the desired resilience; infrastructure automation is not automatically a database backup or code delivery pipeline. This item tests application secret rotation rather than that alternative mechanism.

25. How can you secure access to an S3 bucket from a specific application?
Bank ID: H025 | Official task: 1.1 | Review: Review only
Included in scored practice: No

A. Configure IAM policies
B. Set up VPC Endpoints
C. Use a bucket policy
D. Enable versioning

Book answer: C
Reviewed answer: No unique defensible answer as written
Review rationale: Both identity-based IAM policies/roles and S3 bucket policies can authorize access. A bucket policy is a resource policy, not the only possible method. The original wording does not specify a cross-account or resource-policy requirement, so it does not support declaring every alternative wrong.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Review note. IAM expresses who can perform supported actions on resources. Authorization does not by itself encrypt traffic, record all application logs or route network packets. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
B: Review note. VPC endpoints provide private paths to supported services. IAM and TLS requirements still apply; interface endpoints and gateway endpoints have different costs and capabilities. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
C: Review note. S3 resource policies and access-point policies scope supported object access. A policy is not encryption and does not automatically revoke every separate applicable allow. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
D: Review note. Versioning preserves object versions to recover overwritten/deleted data. It does not encrypt bytes, lower transfer costs or create cross-Region copies by itself. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: bucket authorization ambiguity - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/bucket-policies.html

B. AWS Shield
C. AWS IAM
D. AWS Config

B: Less suitable for this question. Shield addresses distributed denial-of-service attacks, not general IAM authorization, key management or every application exploit. This item tests HTTP attack filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. IAM expresses who can perform supported actions on resources. Authorization does not by itself encrypt traffic, record all application logs or route network packets. This item tests HTTP attack filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Config records supported resource configurations and evaluates rules; configured remediation is separate. It is detective rather than a universal preventive permission firewall. This item tests HTTP attack filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.

28. How can you restrict which AWS services can be used within an AWS Organization?
Bank ID: H028 | Official task: 1.1 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Use Service Control Policies (SCPs)
B. Create IAM Roles
C. Configure Security Groups
D. Use VPC Flow Logs

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: Organizations SCPs bound maximum permissions for principals in affected member accounts. They do not grant permissions and do not restrict the management account in the same way. Local administrator allows cannot override applicable SCP denies.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. Organizations SCPs bound maximum permissions for principals in affected member accounts. They do not grant permissions and do not restrict the management account in the same way. Local administrator allows cannot override applicable SCP denies.
B: Less suitable for this question. IAM expresses who can perform supported actions on resources. Authorization does not by itself encrypt traffic, record all application logs or route network packets. This item tests organization permission guardrails rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. Security groups provide stateful interface-level allow rules for network traffic. They do not have explicit deny rules or implement application user permissions. This item tests organization permission guardrails rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. VPC Flow Logs record supported traffic metadata rather than complete payloads or the IAM identity that edited a security policy. This item tests organization permission guardrails rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: organization permission guardrails - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/organizations/latest/userguide/orgs_manage_policies_scps.html

29. Which feature helps to ensure that IAM credentials are not shared or leaked?
Bank ID: H029 | Official task: 1.1 | Review: Review only
Included in scored practice: No

A. Rotate access keys regularly
B. Enable CloudTrail
C. Configure Security Groups
D. Use VPC Endpoints

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: No unique defensible answer as written
Review rationale: Key rotation limits the lifetime of exposed long-term keys but cannot ensure credentials are never leaked. MFA protects interactive authentication; assumed roles reduce long-lived key distribution. The source overstates guarantees and offers overlapping security practices.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Review note. Rotate or remove long-lived keys and prefer temporary role credentials where possible. Rotation limits exposure but cannot guarantee a key is never leaked. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
B: Review note. CloudTrail records supported account API activity under configured event coverage. It is not ordinary application performance monitoring or a full packet capture. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
C: Review note. Security groups provide stateful interface-level allow rules for network traffic. They do not have explicit deny rules or implement application user permissions. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
D: Review note. VPC endpoints provide private paths to supported services. IAM and TLS requirements still apply; interface endpoints and gateway endpoints have different costs and capabilities. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: credential hygiene - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/IAM/latest/UserGuide/id_roles.html

Included in scored practice: No

A. AWS Config
B. AWS Organizations
C. AWS Trusted Advisor
D. AWS CloudWatch

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: No unique defensible answer as written
Review rationale: Organizations supports account organization and policy guardrails, but Config can evaluate compliance and WAF policies can govern particular workloads. The broad word compliance does not distinguish preventive authorization from detective resource checks.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Review note. Config records supported resource configurations and evaluates rules; configured remediation is separate. It is detective rather than a universal preventive permission firewall. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
B: Review note. Organizations manages accounts and policies; SCPs constrain member-account permissions but do not grant them or run backups. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
C: Review note. Trusted Advisor provides supported best-practice and cost checks. It recommends actions rather than automatically eliminating every unused resource. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
D: Review note. Metrics, logs and alarms observe behavior and can trigger configured actions. Monitoring alone does not scale resources, enforce identity permissions, encrypt data or create replicas. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.

33. What AWS service can be used to create a secure, isolated network for running AWS resources?
Bank ID: H033 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS VPC
B. AWS Direct Connect
C. AWS VPN
D. AWS Transit Gateway

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: A VPC is a logically isolated virtual network with configurable subnets, routes and network security controls. Connectivity services attach networks, while compute services run applications inside network configurations rather than defining that network themselves.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. A VPC is a logically isolated virtual network with configurable subnets, routes and network security controls. Connectivity services attach networks, while compute services run applications inside network configurations rather than defining that network themselves.
B: Less suitable for this question. Direct Connect supplies dedicated private hybrid connectivity, not encryption by default. A private circuit is not a general internet CDN or migration tool by itself. This item tests VPC network isolation rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. VPN establishes encrypted tunnels over a network path. It does not provide DNS, cryptographic key administration or application deployment. This item tests VPC network isolation rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Transit Gateway interconnects routed networks through a hub. It requires routing/segmentation design and can add attachment and processing costs. This item tests VPC network isolation rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: VPC network isolation - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/vpc/latest/userguide/what-is-amazon-vpc.html

B. Managing IAM policies
C. Encrypting data at rest
D. Configuring VPCs

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: AWS Shield provides DDoS protections. Shield Standard is automatic, while Shield Advanced adds paid capabilities. WAF focuses on HTTP request rules, and monitoring or access policies do not substitute for DDoS mitigation.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. AWS Shield provides DDoS protections. Shield Standard is automatic, while Shield Advanced adds paid capabilities. WAF focuses on HTTP request rules, and monitoring or access policies do not substitute for DDoS mitigation.
B: Less suitable for this question. IAM expresses who can perform supported actions on resources. Authorization does not by itself encrypt traffic, record all application logs or route network packets. This item tests DDoS mitigation rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. Stored-data encryption and TLS protect different data paths; encryption does not replace replication, query capacity or traffic distribution. This item tests DDoS mitigation rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Network isolation and routing are distinct from managed compute, encryption and resource authorization; no isolated network alone supplies all those properties. This item tests DDoS mitigation rather than that alternative mechanism.

38. What AWS service provides centralized management of network access control lists?
Bank ID: H038 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Corrected
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS VPC
B. AWS CloudFront
C. AWS Route 53
D. AWS Security Hub

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: Network ACLs are configured as VPC networking resources. Calling this centralized management is imprecise: VPC does not itself provide a global organization-wide ACL manager. The practice stem asks where subnet network ACLs are configured. Practice stem clarified; original preserved below.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. Network ACLs are configured as VPC networking resources. Calling this centralized management is imprecise: VPC does not itself provide a global organization-wide ACL manager. The practice stem asks where subnet network ACLs are configured.
B: Less suitable for this question. CloudFront delivers and caches supported HTTP content at edge locations. It does not deploy regional compute, optimize SQL or provide arbitrary raw TCP acceleration. This item tests VPC network ACL configuration rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. Route 53 provides authoritative DNS and supported routing policies. Each policy has distinct selection rules; DNS routing does not provision application copies or replicate data. This item tests VPC network ACL configuration rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Security Hub aggregates supported security findings and posture checks; it does not replace all source detectors or automatically remediate every issue. This item tests VPC network ACL configuration rather than that alternative mechanism.

40. To create a secure, isolated environment for your cloud applications, which AWS service should you use?
Bank ID: H040 | Official task: 1.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. AWS VPC
B. AWS Elastic Beanstalk
C. AWS Lambda
D. AWS ECS

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: A VPC is a logically isolated virtual network with configurable subnets, routes and network security controls. Connectivity services attach networks, while compute services run applications inside network configurations rather than defining that network themselves.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. A VPC is a logically isolated virtual network with configurable subnets, routes and network security controls. Connectivity services attach networks, while compute services run applications inside network configurations rather than defining that network themselves.
B: Less suitable for this question. Elastic Beanstalk manages supported application environments and deployments. HA needs an appropriate multi-instance load-balanced configuration; a managed single instance remains a failure point. This item tests VPC network isolation rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. Lambda executes bounded event-driven functions with automatic concurrency scaling subject to limits. It is not persistent storage, general container orchestration or a universal substitute for long-running server workloads. This item tests VPC network isolation rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. ECS schedules container services and tasks. The choice of Fargate or EC2 capacity affects host management; generic Docker packaging alone does not distinguish ECS from Kubernetes. This item tests VPC network isolation rather than that alternative mechanism.

41. What is the purpose of AWS KMS (Key Management Service)?
Bank ID: H041 | Official task: 1.3 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. To manage encryption keys for securing data
B. To create and manage VPC configurations
C. To monitor API activity
D. To handle resource scaling

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: KMS manages cryptographic keys and integrates with services such as DynamoDB for encryption at rest. Secrets Manager stores secret values, ACM handles certificates, and WAF filters web requests. Encryption does not replace access authorization.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. KMS manages cryptographic keys and integrates with services such as DynamoDB for encryption at rest. Secrets Manager stores secret values, ACM handles certificates, and WAF filters web requests. Encryption does not replace access authorization.
B: Less suitable for this question. VPC defines isolated virtual networking, routes and subnet boundaries. It does not automatically make every appliance or application dependency redundant. This item tests managed encryption keys rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. CloudTrail records supported account API activity under configured event coverage. It is not ordinary application performance monitoring or a full packet capture. This item tests managed encryption keys rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. EC2 Auto Scaling changes and maintains group instance capacity using configured policies. It does not simply resize live instances vertically, act as a load balancer or operate one group across Regions. This item tests managed encryption keys rather than that alternative mechanism.

42. How can you limit access to a specific AWS resource using IAM?
Bank ID: H042 | Official task: 1.1 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Use IAM policies
B. Configure Security Groups
C. Apply encryption
D. Set up CloudWatch alarms

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: IAM policies express allowed or denied actions on resources, subject to conditions and policy evaluation. Roles carry permissions and are assumed by trusted principals. Network controls cannot substitute for resource/action authorization.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. IAM policies express allowed or denied actions on resources, subject to conditions and policy evaluation. Roles carry permissions and are assumed by trusted principals. Network controls cannot substitute for resource/action authorization.
B: Less suitable for this question. Security groups provide stateful interface-level allow rules for network traffic. They do not have explicit deny rules or implement application user permissions. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. Encryption protects data confidentiality under a key/transport model. It does not itself recover deleted objects, distribute traffic, scale capacity or prove the caller is authorized. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Metrics, logs and alarms observe behavior and can trigger configured actions. Monitoring alone does not scale resources, enforce identity permissions, encrypt data or create replicas. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.

D: Review note. VPC endpoints provide private paths to supported services. IAM and TLS requirements still apply; interface endpoints and gateway endpoints have different costs and capabilities. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.

Included in scored practice: Yes

B. Network ACLs
C. VPC Endpoints
D. NAT Gateways

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: A
Review rationale: Security groups allow specified protocols, ports and source/destination CIDRs or security groups at network interfaces. They are stateful and have no explicit deny rules; omit unwanted sources and audit all attached groups.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. Security groups allow specified protocols, ports and source/destination CIDRs or security groups at network interfaces. They are stateful and have no explicit deny rules; omit unwanted sources and audit all attached groups.
B: Less suitable for this question. Network ACLs provide stateless subnet-boundary allow/deny filtering with ordered rules. Return paths must be permitted explicitly; they do not inspect application payloads. This item tests stateful resource traffic filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. VPC endpoints provide private paths to supported services. IAM and TLS requirements still apply; interface endpoints and gateway endpoints have different costs and capabilities. This item tests stateful resource traffic filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. NAT enables specified outbound address translation for private resources. It is not an inbound authorization system, a global routing service or a data encryption feature. This item tests stateful resource traffic filtering rather than that alternative mechanism.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Correct. IAM policies express allowed or denied actions on resources, subject to conditions and policy evaluation. Roles carry permissions and are assumed by trusted principals. Network controls cannot substitute for resource/action authorization.
B: Less suitable for this question. Security groups provide stateful interface-level allow rules for network traffic. They do not have explicit deny rules or implement application user permissions. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Less suitable for this question. VPC endpoints provide private paths to supported services. IAM and TLS requirements still apply; interface endpoints and gateway endpoints have different costs and capabilities. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Metrics, logs and alarms observe behavior and can trigger configured actions. Monitoring alone does not scale resources, enforce identity permissions, encrypt data or create replicas. This item tests identity authorization rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: identity authorization - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/IAM/latest/UserGuide/id_roles.html

D: Less suitable for this question. VPC peering connects compatible VPCs and is not transitive. It is not an on-premises VPN, encryption feature or application credential service. This item tests private AWS service endpoints rather than that alternative mechanism.

B. To provide DDoS protection

D: Review note. IAM expresses who can perform supported actions on resources. Authorization does not by itself encrypt traffic, record all application logs or route network packets. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.

Review rationale: Storage Gateway supplies hybrid storage interfaces, while AWS Backup centrally orchestrates supported backups including supported hybrid resources. Reasons for each original option:
A: Review note. Storage Gateway exposes hybrid file, volume or tape interfaces backed by AWS storage. It is not a universal cloud database or automatic failover service for arbitrary on-premises applications. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
B: Review note. The Glacier storage classes target archival patterns with class-specific retrieval behavior. The generic name does not specify Instant, Flexible or Deep Archive performance/costs. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
C: Review note. Backups supply recovery points and supported restore workflows. Restoration takes time; backup retention does not provide immediate live failover or replace every data replication design. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
D: Review note. CloudFormation provisions and updates declared resource stacks from templates. A template must encode the desired resilience; infrastructure automation is not automatically a database backup or code delivery pipeline. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.

D: Less suitable for this question. Route 53 provides authoritative DNS and supported routing policies. Each policy has distinct selection rules; DNS routing does not provision application copies or replicate data. This item tests DNS health-based failover rather than that alternative mechanism.

D: Less suitable for this question. Transit Gateway interconnects routed networks through a hub. It requires routing/segmentation design and can add attachment and processing costs. This item tests encrypted hybrid tunnels rather than that alternative mechanism.

199. What feature of AWS S3 ensures that data is replicated to multiple geographically separated locations?
Bank ID: H199 | Official task: 2.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. S3 Object Lock
B. S3 Cross-Region Replication
C. S3 Intelligent-Tiering
D. S3 Glacier Deep Archive

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: Configured S3 Cross-Region Replication copies eligible objects to a destination in another Region. Versioning and permissions are required, existing objects need an appropriate batch replication path, and replication is asynchronous rather than a zero-loss failover guarantee.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. Object Lock prevents deletion/overwriting of retained protected object versions under the selected mode. It is not the feature that creates every version or an access-frequency optimizer. This item tests geographic object replication rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. Configured S3 Cross-Region Replication copies eligible objects to a destination in another Region. Versioning and permissions are required, existing objects need an appropriate batch replication path, and replication is asynchronous rather than a zero-loss failover guarantee.
C: Less suitable for this question. Intelligent-Tiering moves eligible S3 objects between access tiers based on observed access; optional archival tiers require configuration and have restore trade-offs. This item tests geographic object replication rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. S3 Glacier Deep Archive targets very cold long-duration retention and needs asynchronous restoration. Minimum duration and retrieval charges matter; it is not for immediate reads. This item tests geographic object replication rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: geographic object replication - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/userguide/replication.html

A. Increased write throughput
B. High availability
C. Improved read performance
D. Enhanced data security

Book answer: C
Reviewed answer: C
Review rationale: RDS read replicas serve eligible read queries and typically replicate asynchronously. They do not increase the primary's write capacity, and replica lag affects freshness. Promotion and cross-Region replicas can help recovery, but read scaling is their main distinguishing purpose here.

A: Less suitable for this question. Read replicas offload reads; primary writes still go to the writer. This item tests read replica offload rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Less suitable for this question. Replicas can assist recovery, but read performance is their main distinguishing purpose; synchronous standby HA is a different deployment. This item tests read replica offload rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Correct. RDS read replicas serve eligible read queries and typically replicate asynchronously. They do not increase the primary's write capacity, and replica lag affects freshness. Promotion and cross-Region replicas can help recovery, but read scaling is their main distinguishing purpose here.
D: Less suitable for this question. Replicas are not primarily a data-access or encryption security feature. This item tests read replica offload rather than that alternative mechanism.

213. Which AWS service provides a scalable file storage solution for use with Amazon EC2 instances?
Bank ID: H213 | Official task: 3.1 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. Amazon S3
B. Amazon EFS
C. Amazon Glacier
D. AWS Storage Gateway

Book answer: B
Reviewed answer: B
Review rationale: EFS provides elastic shared NFS file storage for supported Linux clients, including appropriately connected on-premises clients. Regional and One Zone deployments have different resilience properties. S3 is object storage and EBS is AZ-scoped block storage.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. S3 is durable object storage. It is not a drop-in relational database, block volume, shared NFS file system or general execution engine. This item tests shared managed NFS storage rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Correct. EFS provides elastic shared NFS file storage for supported Linux clients, including appropriately connected on-premises clients. Regional and One Zone deployments have different resilience properties. S3 is object storage and EBS is AZ-scoped block storage.
C: Less suitable for this question. The Glacier storage classes target archival patterns with class-specific retrieval behavior. The generic name does not specify Instant, Flexible or Deep Archive performance/costs. This item tests shared managed NFS storage rather than that alternative mechanism.
D: Less suitable for this question. Storage Gateway exposes hybrid file, volume or tape interfaces backed by AWS storage. It is not a universal cloud database or automatic failover service for arbitrary on-premises applications. This item tests shared managed NFS storage rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: shared managed NFS storage - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/efs/latest/ug/whatisefs.html

214. What type of Amazon EC2 instance is best suited for compute-intensive applications?
Bank ID: H214 | Official task: 3.2 | Review: Reviewed
Included in scored practice: Yes

A. T Series
B. M Series
C. C Series
D. R Series

Book answer: C
Reviewed answer: C
Review rationale: EC2 C-family instances are compute optimized for CPU-intensive workloads. T-family instances are burstable, M-family instances are general purpose, and R-family instances are memory optimized. Benchmark actual requirements rather than selecting only by family name.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Less suitable for this question. EC2 T families provide burstable performance and credit-based behavior, which differs from sustained compute-optimized demand. This item tests compute-optimized instances rather than that alternative mechanism.
B: Less suitable for this question. EC2 M families balance compute, memory and network resources for general-purpose workloads. This item tests compute-optimized instances rather than that alternative mechanism.
C: Correct. EC2 C-family instances are compute optimized for CPU-intensive workloads. T-family instances are burstable, M-family instances are general purpose, and R-family instances are memory optimized. Benchmark actual requirements rather than selecting only by family name.
D: Less suitable for this question. EC2 R families are memory optimized for large working sets; they are not the general default for a CPU-bound workload. This item tests compute-optimized instances rather than that alternative mechanism.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: compute-optimized instances - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AWSEC2/latest/UserGuide/concepts.html

215. How can you improve the read performance of an Amazon DynamoDB table?
Bank ID: H215 | Official task: 3.3 | Review: Review only
Included in scored practice: No

A. Increase the read capacity units
B. Enable DynamoDB Streams
C. Use Amazon ElastiCache
D. Implement DynamoDB Global Tables

Book answer: A
Reviewed answer: No unique defensible answer as written
Review rationale: Higher read capacity helps when provisioned capacity is insufficient, caching can reduce repeated reads, and global tables can reduce regional distance for distributed users. Streams expose changes rather than increasing read capacity. The missing access pattern prevents a unique performance recommendation.

Reasons for each original option:
A: Review note. Provisioned read/write capacity affects DynamoDB throughput for the relevant operation type. Extra write capacity does not fix insufficient read capacity, and vice versa. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
B: Review note. DynamoDB Streams publishes item change records for processing; it is not directly a provisioned read-capacity increase or synchronous RDS failover feature. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
C: Review note. ElastiCache supplies managed in-memory data stores. Caching can offload repeated reads but requires consistency, expiration and authoritative-data decisions. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.
D: Review note. DynamoDB global tables provide configured multi-Region replicas. Application routing and the selected consistency/conflict model still matter; they are not a recovery mechanism for every database engine. The source wording does not make this option uniquely wrong; see the item review above.

AWS references:
AWS documentation: DynamoDB read bottleneck ambiguity - https://docs.aws.amazon.com/amazondynamodb/latest/developerguide/Introduction.html
